Per mark, `onProgress` → `renderProgressLine()` (js/app.js:510–517/313–320) rewrites the active `.status-progress` textContent. State-line swaps are pure CSS visibility flips (`body[data-state]` → display) — zero DOM mutations inside the region; the per-mark rewrites are its ONLY mutation-driven announcement stream.
  - **Runtime cadence (fresh-profile headless Chrome 152.0.7977.64 CDP fake-torch rig — canvas-captureStream track, torch caps, resolving applyConstraints recorder, permissions→prompt, throwing storage guards, trusted `Input.dispatchMouseEvent`/`insertText`, 390×844; MutationObserver on the station subtree):** SOS @ LEARNER = 10 childList mutations (double MARK-1 seed + marks 1–9), sustained-dot intervals 477–488 ms ≈ **2.1 Hz** (exactly `wpm/2.4`, schedule.js); SOS @ BRISK = 10 mutations, sustained-dot intervals 193–205 ms = **5.0 Hz**. The default 38-mark run = 38 candidates. With `atomic=true` each candidate announces the FULL station (~2–3 s speech) every 0.2–1.4 s: VoiceOver's polite queue grows faster than it drains (stale ledger, DONE buried — WCAG 4.1.3 timeliness defeated); NVDA interrupts per mutation (~38 syllable fragments). The critique's question — "status, or a dot-by-dot ledger?" — answers: a dot-by-dot ledger.
  - **Engine grounding (why the fix works, beyond spec intent):** Chromium `ax_object.cc` — `LiveRegionStatus()` returns the EXPLICIT aria-live value first (including "off"); `IsLiveRegionRoot()` is true for any non-empty value (source comment: a root "can be from any ARIA live value, including 'off'"); cached root = `IsLiveRegionRoot() ? this : parent_->LiveRegionRoot()`. Mutations inside an off node resolve to their own OFF root, never the polite container. Gecko: same via container-live object attributes. The fix is monotonic: worst case on any engine = the previous behavior, never worse.
- **Fix (files: index.html, test/live-region.test.mjs new):** `aria-live="off"` on the five `.status-progress` lines (broadcasting / broadcasting-on-screen / paused / paused-on-screen / error) + a comment stating the law (states announce; a per-mark ledger never does). The 15 `.status-line` elements deliberately keep NO aria-live of their own — they inherit the station's polite channel, so SENDING/PAUSED/takeover/DONE/FAULT announce exactly as before, and atomic state-change reads still carry the current MARK position (e.g. PAUSED at MARK 8 OF 38). DESIGN.md's "aria-live polite" Status Station contract is untouched. `test/live-region.test.mjs` (5 tests, speed-freeze precedent): only-live-region invariant; five-off scoping with exact state datasets; no state-line overrides; the visual per-mark render path pinned unchanged (`onProgress` → `renderProgressLine()`; no aria-live manipulation in app.js); non-vacuous detector self-test (pre-fix shape AND a one-line-missed partial fix both flagged).
- **Validation:** `npm test` **494/494 pass, 0 fail** (489 + 5 new). Post-fix CDP re-run (same rig class, fresh profile): the visual line still updates EVERY mark at both speeds (10/10 writes per run, "MARK 1 OF 9" → "MARK 9 OF 9" distinct and in order, intervals still exactly the schedule); mid-broadcast DOM probe — 5/5 progress lines `aria-live="off"`, station still `role="status" aria-live="polite"`, 0/15 state lines with own aria-live; **zero console events/exceptions**; one gUM per arm with the committed constraints; torch applies normal both directions. Detector `detect.mjs --json index.html`: this session's fully-parsed scan shows 22 pre-existing findings (the critique's zero-findings run was regex-degraded and disclosed itself as an undercount; I5/Q2 adjudicated the design-record features it lists — stencil tracking, lamp glows, tape material shading, transport key sets). **A/B proof of no detector delta:** an in-repo sed-stripped pre-fix copy (aria-live removed, deleted immediately after) vs the shipped file — **zero findings added, zero removed** by this change.
- **Scope discipline:** js/app.js, js/conductor.js (T-refine-4's verified mtimes), js/copy.js, css/main.css, js/states.js, js/torch.js all untouched; no world/token/copy changes; no new dependencies; rig in /tmp only — zero repo residue.

The pins are live.
- **Engine grounding re-derived from source, not the log:** Chromium main `third_party/blink/renderer/modules/accessibility/ax_object.cc` fetched and read by the verifier — `LiveRegionStatus()` returns the **explicit** `aria-live` attribute value FIRST (any value, including "off", before implicit role values); `IsLiveRegionRoot()` is `!LiveRegionStatus().empty()` (TRUE for "off"); `IsActiveLiveRegionRoot()` excludes "off" (an off root exists but never announces); the cached-root computation resolves `IsLiveRegionRoot() ? this : parent_->LiveRegionRoot()` under the literal source comment *"Compute live region root, which can be from any ARIA live value, including 'off'"*. So every mutation inside an `aria-live="off"` paragraph resolves to its OWN off root and never reaches the polite station — the fix's mechanism is real in the engine, and monotonicity holds (an engine that ignored explicit off would reproduce exactly the previous behavior).
- **Verifier discovery (recorded for future auditors):** CDP's `Accessibility.getFullAXTree` serialization **omits a `live` property when its value is "off"** — proven with a control data-URL rig (`<p aria-live="off">` serializes with empty properties exactly like a plain `<p>`, while `aria-live="polite"` and `role="status"` serialize live/atomic/relevant). Consequence: a naive "live node census" cannot SEE off regions; the correct engine observables are (1) census = the station only (any polite/assertive/implicit-live region WOULD serialize), (2) the off paragraph present in the AX tree with its MARK text, (3) the explicit DOM attribute `aria-live="off"`.
- **Mechanics (verifier's own AX probes):** the station resolves as `role=status` with computed `live=polite`, `atomic=true`, `relevant="additions text"` and is the ONLY serialized live node at idle, mid-broadcast, mid-takeover, done, and across all five progress-bearing states (each materialized via a pure `body[data-state]` CSS flip): no `alert`/`log`/`marquee`/`timer` roles anywhere; the page's aria-live attributes total exactly 6 (station polite + the five off); 15/15 `.status-line` elements carry no aria-live of their own. Each of the five `.status-progress` paragraphs materializes in the AX tree in its state (MARK text present) with explicit DOM `aria-live="off"` — per the source above, its own off root.
- **Behavior (trusted-input journey: SOS @ LEARNER, torch channel → forced-loss takeover → done):** the visible per-mark line rewrites EVERY mark — **MARK 1→9 OF 9 distinct, in order**, sustained-dot write deltas 477–488 ms (**2.1 Hz**, exactly `wpm/2.4`) — I2 visual parity unchanged; all **11** station-subtree DOM mutations across the whole journey are `.status-progress` textContent writes (state-line swaps contribute **0** mutations — pure CSS flips, confirming the audit's mechanics claim); between two consecutive marks the station-subtree AX delta is confined to the MARK text (no state-line churn). State transitions still announce through the polite station: **idle→preflight** and **broadcasting-on-screen→done** are AX ADDITIONS/REMOVALS of the state lines inside the polite root (the announcement-candidate class), with the station's rendered text swapping `IDLE…`→`PREFLIGHT…`→…→`DONE THE RUN IS OVER…` verbatim. **Takeover announces via its state line:** the reject lever mid-run → `broadcasting-on-screen`, exactly ONE rejected apply and ZERO torch applies after it, the note rendered with exact copy (`LIGHT SOURCE LOST — CONTINUING ON SCREEN. POSITION KEPT. THE MESSAGE LOSES NOTHING.`), and the station's rendered text carrying `SENDING SCREEN LIGHT SPEAKS THE TAPE.` + `MARK 3 OF 9 · LEARNER — 5 WPM` — the current position rides the atomic state-change read.
